====RecallDemo====
     1	import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
     2	import { AnimatePresence, motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
     3	import { Mic, Pause, Play } from "lucide-react";
     4	
     5	/**
     6	 * The method, performed.
     7	 *
     8	 * A looping demonstration of one stimulus worked the way the drill asks: each
     9	 * piece read, told back out loud, coloured, and then the whole argument
    10	 * recalled with the text gone. It plays on the door because the one thing that
    11	 * makes an unfamiliar procedure click is watching somebody do it once, and
    12	 * because the procedure's strangest moment -- the stimulus vanishing -- is
    13	 * better seen than described.
    14	 *
    15	 * Everything here is synthetic and says so. The stimulus is written for the
    16	 * demo, the transcript is authored, the clock is a prop. Nothing on it is a
    17	 * measurement of anyone.
    18	 */
    19	
    20	const STIMULUS = [
    21	  "City planners argue that widening Elm Street will ease congestion.",
    22	  "But in the three cities that widened comparable roads, traffic rose by roughly a fifth within two years.",
    23	  "So widening Elm Street will probably make congestion worse, not better.",
    24	];
    25	
    26	const TELL = [
    27	  "So the planners want to widen Elm Street because they think it will cut the traffic.",
    28	  "But three other cities did the same thing and traffic actually went up, like twenty percent, over two years.",
    29	  "So the author is saying widening it will probably make things worse.",
    30	];
    31	
    32	const COLOUR = ["Classic. Every city says that.", "Twenty percent is a lot. Who counted?", "Probably. Not definitely."];
    33	
    34	const RECALL =
    35	  "Planners say widen Elm Street to ease congestion. Three cities widened similar roads and traffic went up about a fifth. So it will probably make it worse.";
    36	
    37	const DETAILS: { label: string; carried: boolean }[] = [
    38	  { label: "Elm Street", carried: true },
    39	  { label: "planners", carried: true },
    40	  { label: "ease congestion", carried: true },
    41	  { label: "three cities", carried: true },
    42	  { label: "up a fifth", carried: true },
    43	  { label: "within two years", carried: false },
    44	  { label: "worse, not better", carried: true },
    45	];
    46	
    47	type Kind = "read" | "tell" | "colour" | "space" | "recall" | "tally" | "hold";
    48	interface Beat {
    49	  kind: Kind;
    50	  i: number;
    51	  ms: number;
    52	}
    53	
    54	const BEATS: Beat[] = [
    55	  ...[0, 1, 2].flatMap((i): Beat[] => [
    56	    { kind: "read", i, ms: 1900 },
    57	    { kind: "tell", i, ms: 2600 },
    58	    { kind: "colour", i, ms: 1500 },
    59	  ]),
    60	  { kind: "space", i: 3, ms: 900 },
    61	  { kind: "recall", i: 3, ms: 4200 },
    62	  { kind: "tally", i: 3, ms: 1900 },
    63	  { kind: "hold", i: 3, ms: 2600 },
    64	];
    65	
    66	const STEP_LABEL: Record<Kind, string> = {
    67	  read: "01 · Read it",
    68	  tell: "02 · Tell it back",
    69	  colour: "03 · Colour it",
    70	  space: "Space",
    71	  recall: "04 · Recall it",
    72	  tally: "Carried",
    73	  hold: "Carried",
    74	};
    75	
    76	/**
    77	 * Reveals `text` a character at a time across `ms`, starting over each time
    78	 * `on` flips true. When `on` goes false the text stays where it got to, so a
    79	 * retelling remains readable while the colour lands beside it.
    80	 */
    81	function useTypewriter(text: string, on: boolean, ms: number, instant: boolean): string {
    82	  const [n, setN] = useState(instant ? text.length : 0);
    83	  useEffect(() => {
    84	    if (instant) {
    85	      setN(text.length);
    86	      return;
    87	    }
    88	    if (!on) return;
    89	    let raf = 0;
    90	    const t0 = performance.now();
    91	    setN(0);
    92	    const tick = (t: number) => {
    93	      const k = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / ms);
    94	      setN(Math.floor(k * text.length));
    95	      if (k < 1) raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    96	    };
    97	    raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    98	    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    99	  }, [text, on, ms, instant]);
   100	  return text.slice(0, n);
   101	}
   102	
   103	function Waveform({ live }: { live: boolean }) {
   104	  return (
   105	    <span className="rd-wave" data-live={live || undefined} aria-hidden="true">
   106	      {Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => (
   107	        <i key={i} style={{ animationDelay: `${(i * 0.09).toFixed(2)}s` }} />
   108	      ))}
   109	    </span>
   110	  );
   111	}
   112	
   113	function clock(seconds: number): string {
   114	  const m = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
   115	  const s = seconds % 60;
   116	  return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, "0")}`;
   117	}
   118	
   119	export function RecallDemo() {
   120	  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
   121	  const [idx, setIdx] = useState(0);
   122	  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(true);
   123	  const [seconds, setSeconds] = useState(0);
   124	  const timer = useRef<number | null>(null);
   125	
   126	  const running = playing && !reduced;
   127	
   128	  useEffect(() => {
   129	    if (!running) return;
   130	    timer.current = window.setTimeout(() => setIdx((i) => (i + 1) % BEATS.length), BEATS[idx].ms);
   131	    return () => {
   132	      if (timer.current) window.clearTimeout(timer.current);
   133	    };
   134	  }, [idx, running]);
   135	
   136	  useEffect(() => {
   137	    if (!running) return;
   138	    const id = window.setInterval(() => setSeconds((s) => (s + 1) % 1500), 1000);
   139	    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
   140	  }, [running]);
   141	
   142	  // Reduced motion: the finished state, held, with nothing moving.
   143	  const beat = reduced ? BEATS[BEATS.length - 1] : BEATS[idx];
   144	  const phase = beat.kind;
   145	  const sentence = beat.i;
   146	  const gone = phase === "recall" || phase === "tally" || phase === "hold";
   147	  const spacePressed = phase === "space";
   148	
   149	  const tellText = useTypewriter(TELL[Math.min(sentence, 2)], phase === "tell", 2000, Boolean(reduced));
   150	  const recallText = useTypewriter(RECALL, phase === "recall", 3600, Boolean(reduced));
   151	  const showTally = phase === "tally" || phase === "hold";
   152	  const carried = DETAILS.filter((d) => d.carried).length;
   153	
   154	  return (
   155	    <div className="rd-demo" data-phase={phase}>
   156	      <div className="rd-demo-bar">
   157	        <span className="rd-demo-dots" aria-hidden="true">
   158	          <i />
   159	          <i />
   160	          <i />
   161	        </span>
   162	        <span className="rd-demo-title">Read, Recall, Colour</span>
   163	        <span className="rd-demo-step" aria-live="polite">
   164	          {STEP_LABEL[phase]}
   165	        </span>
   166	        <span className="rd-demo-rec">
   167	          <i className="rd-rec-dot" data-live={running || undefined} aria-hidden="true" />
   168	          <span className="rd-demo-clock">{clock(seconds)}</span>
   169	        </span>
   170	        <button
   171	          type="button"
   172	          className="rd-demo-play"
   173	          onClick={() => setPlaying((p) => !p)}
   174	          aria-label={playing ? "Pause the demonstration" : "Play the demonstration"}
   175	          disabled={Boolean(reduced)}
   176	        >
   177	          {playing && !reduced ? <Pause size={13} strokeWidth={2.4} /> : <Play size={13} strokeWidth={2.4} />}
   178	        </button>
   179	      </div>
   180	
   181	      <div className="rd-demo-body">
   182	        <div className="rd-paper" data-gone={gone || undefined}>
   183	          <span className="rd-demo-label">Stimulus</span>
   184	          <p className="rd-stim">
   185	            {STIMULUS.map((s, i) => (
   186	              <span
   187	                key={i}
   188	                className="rd-stim-line"
   189	                data-active={!gone && i === sentence ? "true" : undefined}
   190	                data-done={!gone && i < sentence ? "true" : undefined}
   191	              >
   192	                {s}{" "}
   193	              </span>
   194	            ))}
   195	          </p>
   196	          <AnimatePresence>
   197	            {gone && (
   198	              <motion.p
   199	                className="rd-gone"
   200	                initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 6 }}
   201	                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
   202	                exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
   203	                transition={{ duration: 0.5, delay: 0.5, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
   204	              >
   205	                The stimulus is gone. Say the whole argument back from memory.
   206	              </motion.p>
   207	            )}
   208	          </AnimatePresence>
   209	          <div className="rd-keyrow" aria-hidden="true">
   210	            <span className="rd-key" data-pressed={spacePressed || undefined}>
   211	              space
   212	            </span>
   213	            <span className="rd-keyhint">{gone ? "once more to move on" : "when you finish the piece"}</span>
   214	          </div>
   215	        </div>
   216	
   217	        <div className="rd-voice">
   218	          <span className="rd-demo-label">Out loud</span>
   219	          <div className="rd-voice-stack">
   220	            <AnimatePresence mode="popLayout" initial={false}>
   221	              {!gone && (phase === "tell" || phase === "colour") && (
   222	                <motion.div
   223	                  key={`tell-${sentence}`}
   224	                  className="rd-bubble"
   225	                  layout
   226	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 14, scale: 0.98 }}
   227	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
   228	                  exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10, transition: { duration: 0.25 } }}
   229	                  transition={{ duration: 0.45, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
   230	                >
   231	                  <span className="rd-bubble-head">
   232	                    <Mic size={12} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
   233	                    You say
   234	                    <Waveform live={phase === "tell"} />
   235	                  </span>
   236	                  <p>
   237	                    “{tellText}
   238	                    {phase === "tell" && <span className="rd-caret" aria-hidden="true" />}”
   239	                  </p>
   240	                </motion.div>
   241	              )}
   242	              {!gone && phase === "colour" && (
   243	                <motion.div
   244	                  key={`colour-${sentence}`}
   245	                  className="rd-colour"
   246	                  layout
   247	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, scale: 0.9, rotate: -1.5 }}
   248	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, rotate: 0 }}
   249	                  exit={{ opacity: 0, transition: { duration: 0.2 } }}
   250	                  transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 380, damping: 26 }}
   251	                >
   252	                  <span className="rd-colour-head">Colour it</span>
   253	                  <p>“{COLOUR[sentence]}”</p>
   254	                </motion.div>
   255	              )}
   256	              {gone && (
   257	                <motion.div
   258	                  key="recall"
   259	                  className="rd-bubble rd-bubble-recall"
   260	                  layout
   261	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 14 }}
   262	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
   263	                  exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
   264	                  transition={{ duration: 0.45, delay: 0.35, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
   265	                >
   266	                  <span className="rd-bubble-head">
   267	                    <Mic size={12} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
   268	                    The Recall
   269	                    <Waveform live={phase === "recall"} />
   270	                  </span>
   271	                  <p>
   272	                    “{recallText}
   273	                    {phase === "recall" && <span className="rd-caret" aria-hidden="true" />}”
   274	                  </p>
   275	                </motion.div>
   276	              )}
   277	              {showTally && (
   278	                <motion.div
   279	                  key="tally"
   280	                  className="rd-tally"
   281	                  layout
   282	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
   283	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
   284	                  exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
   285	                  transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
   286	                >
   287	                  <span className="rd-tally-head">
   288	                    <strong>
   289	                      {carried} of {DETAILS.length}
   290	                    </strong>{" "}
   291	                    details carried
   292	                  </span>
   293	                  <ul className="rd-tally-list">
   294	                    {DETAILS.map((d, i) => (
   295	                      <motion.li
   296	                        key={d.label}
   297	                        data-carried={d.carried || undefined}
   298	                        initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
   299	                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
   300	                        transition={{ delay: 0.15 + i * 0.12, duration: 0.3 }}
   301	                      >
   302	                        {d.label}
   303	                      </motion.li>
   304	                    ))}
   305	                  </ul>
   306	                </motion.div>
   307	              )}
   308	              {!gone && phase === "read" && (
   309	                <motion.p
   310	                  key={`quiet-${sentence}`}
   311	                  className="rd-quiet"
   312	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0 }}
   313	                  animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
   314	                  exit={{ opacity: 0, transition: { duration: 0.15 } }}
   315	                  transition={{ duration: 0.4 }}
   316	                >
   317	                  Reading silently. Nothing comes out yet.
   318	                </motion.p>
   319	              )}
   320	            </AnimatePresence>
   321	          </div>
   322	        </div>
   323	      </div>
   324	      <p className="rd-demo-foot">Demonstration. The stimulus and the transcript are written for this page.</p>
   325	    </div>
   326	  );
   327	}

     1	import type React from "react";
     2	import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
     3	import type { ActivityInstructions } from "../api/types";
     4	import { renderInlineMarks } from "../prompt-format";
     5	import "./section-launch.css";
     6	
     7	export interface SectionBriefingPageProps {
     8	  title: string;
     9	  subtitle: string | null;
    10	  instructions: ActivityInstructions;
    11	  onStart: () => void;
    12	  onExit?: () => void;
    13	}
    14	
    15	/**
    16	 * The instructions, on their own page, before anything records.
    17	 *
    18	 * Pressing Start on a section used to open the microphone straight away, and
    19	 * the method was folded into tabs on a page she had already scrolled past. This
    20	 * page is the whole briefing laid out in order, every step numbered and open,
    21	 * with one Start button at the top and the same one at the foot. The written
    22	 * instructions sit under it, closed. Nothing here begins the run.
    23	 */
    24	export function SectionBriefingPage({
    25	  title,
    26	  subtitle,
    27	  instructions,
    28	  onStart,
    29	  onExit,
    30	}: SectionBriefingPageProps) {
    31	  const briefing = instructions.briefing;
    32	  const steps = briefing?.sections.find((s) => (s.steps?.length ?? 0) > 0)?.steps ?? [];
    33	
    34	  const startButton = (label: string) => (
    35	    <button type="button" className="sl-start" onClick={onStart}>
    36	      {label}
    37	      <ArrowRight size={16} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
    38	    </button>
    39	  );
    40	
    41	  return (
    42	    <div className="sl sl-briefing-page">
    43	      <header className="sl-bp-head">
    44	        <div>
    45	          {/* No chip. The title already names the drill, and on the door the
    46	              chip earns its place because the H1 there is the bare name. */}
    47	          <h1 className="sl-title">{title}</h1>
    48	          {(instructions.tagline || subtitle) && (
    49	            <p className="sl-tagline">
    50	              {instructions.tagline ? renderInlineMarks(instructions.tagline, "sl-bp-tag") : subtitle}
    51	            </p>
    52	          )}
    53	        </div>
    54	        <div className="sl-cta">
    55	          {startButton("Start the section")}
    56	          {onExit && (
    57	            <button type="button" className="sl-brief" onClick={onExit}>
    58	              Back
    59	            </button>
    60	          )}
    61	        </div>
    62	      </header>
    63	
    64	      {/* One fact, the one that lowers her shoulders before she reads. That the
    65	          section records in one take with no pause is said at the foot, beside
    66	          the Start she presses after reading; saying it here as well made the
    67	          notice a paragraph she skipped. (An earlier version of this comment
    68	          claimed Step 4 carried it. It did not: the sentence lived only in
    69	          `instructions.rules`, which this page never renders, so for a day the
    70	          page did not say it anywhere.) */}
    71	      {/* No role="status". A live region is for state that changes without a page
    72	          load; this sentence is the same every time, and announcing it as an
    73	          update is noise. */}
    74	      <p className="sl-notice">
    75	        Nothing is recording yet. The microphone opens when you press Start.
    76	      </p>
    77	
    78	      {steps.length > 0 && (
    79	        <section className="sl-bp-steps" aria-label="What you do, step by step">
    80	          <h2 className="sl-bp-h2">What you do, step by step</h2>
    81	          <ol className="sl-bp-list">
    82	            {steps.map((step, i) => (
    83	              <li key={i} style={{ "--i": i } as React.CSSProperties}>
    84	                <span className="sl-bp-n">Step {i + 1}</span>
    85	                <strong>{renderInlineMarks(step.label, `sl-bps-l-${i}`)}</strong>
    86	                <p>{renderInlineMarks(step.body, `sl-bps-b-${i}`)}</p>
    87	              </li>
    88	            ))}
    89	          </ol>
    90	        </section>
    91	      )}
    92	
    93	      {/* The four steps above ARE the instruction. This is the same method told
    94	          again at length, and rendering both open left her reading the method
    95	          twice with no way to tell which one she was meant to follow. Closed by
    96	          default, one click from anyone who wants the long version. */}
    97	      {briefing && (
    98	        <details className="sl-bp-more">
    99	          {/* The summary carries a real heading rather than only looking like one.
   100	              It read as a section title and was absent from the heading outline,
   101	              which is what left the page jumping H2 to H4. */}
   102	          <summary>
   103	            <h2 className="sl-bp-h2">Read the long version</h2>
   104	          </summary>
   105	          <section className="sl-essay sl-bp-essay" aria-label="Full instructions">
   106	          {briefing.lede && <p className="rd-lede">{renderInlineMarks(briefing.lede, "sl-bp-lede")}</p>}
   107	          {briefing.sections.map((section, i) => (
   108	            <section key={i}>
   109	              <h3>{renderInlineMarks(section.heading, `sl-bp-h-${i}`)}</h3>
   110	              {section.body.map((para, j) => (
   111	                <p key={j}>{renderInlineMarks(para, `sl-bp-p-${i}-${j}`)}</p>
   112	              ))}
   113	              {section.steps && (
   114	                <ol>
   115	                  {section.steps.map((step, j) => (
   116	                    <li key={j}>
   117	                      <strong>{renderInlineMarks(step.label, `sl-bp-sl-${i}-${j}`)}</strong>{" "}
   118	                      {renderInlineMarks(step.body, `sl-bp-sb-${i}-${j}`)}
   119	                    </li>
   120	                  ))}
   121	                </ol>
   122	              )}
   123	              {section.demo && (
   124	                <ol>
   125	                  {section.demo.beats.map((b, j) => (
   126	                    <li key={j}>
   127	                      <strong>{b.kind}</strong> {renderInlineMarks(b.text, `sl-bp-d-${i}-${j}`)}
   128	                    </li>
   129	                  ))}
   130	                </ol>
   131	              )}
   132	            </section>
   133	          ))}
   134	          </section>
   135	        </details>
   136	      )}
   137	
   138	      {/* The two facts she needs at the moment she presses Start, said where
   139	          she presses it: it does not pause, and what the time is for. The
   140	          closer is the authored last beat before Start and belongs here too. */}
   141	      <footer className="sl-bp-foot">
   142	        <p className="sl-bp-fact">
   143	          It runs in one take and does not pause. Twenty-five minutes for the whole section is
   144	          the goal you are working toward, not a limit.
   145	        </p>
   146	        {briefing?.closer && (
   147	          <p className="sl-bp-closer">{renderInlineMarks(briefing.closer, "sl-bp-closer")}</p>
   148	        )}
   149	        {/* Says what happens to her, not how the clock works. "Counting up"
   150	            described the mechanism and left her wondering what it counted
   151	            toward. */}
   152	        <p>
   153	          When you are ready, press Start. Recording begins the moment you do, and the timer
   154	          starts with it. The first time, your browser will ask to use your microphone. Allow it.
   155	        </p>
   156	        <div className="sl-cta">{startButton("Start the section")}</div>
   157	      </footer>
   158	    </div>
   159	  );
   160	}
====Harness====
     1	/**
     2	 * A preview harness for the section launch surface. Development only.
     3	 *
     4	 * It exists so the component can be looked at in a real browser, in every state
     5	 * and at every breakpoint, without signing in and without a graded section
     6	 * sitting in the database. Only the network is stubbed: `window.fetch` is
     7	 * replaced before anything imports the Supabase client, so the component, the
     8	 * client helper and the derivation all run exactly as they do in the product.
     9	 *
    10	 * Not routed and not imported by the app. Reached at
    11	 * `/src/dev/section-launch-preview.html` on the dev server. It is not an entry
    12	 * point in the Vite build, which only builds `index.html`, so it never ships.
    13	 *
    14	 * States: ?state=runs|oneRun|empty|failed|noHistory|library  ·  ?theme=light|dark
    15	 */
    16	import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
    17	// The section JSONs deliberately ship an empty instructions block -- the rules
    18	// are authored once, in one file, and copied in at seed time -- so the harness
    19	// reads the same instructions file the migration seeds.
    20	import sectionInstructions from "../features/activity-centre/content/read-recall-colour-section.instructions.json";
    21	import type { ActivityInstructions } from "../features/activity-centre/api/types";
    22	
    23	const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    24	const state = params.get("state") ?? "runs";
    25	const theme = params.get("theme") ?? "light";
    26	
    27	function stim(item_order: number, over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
    28	  return {
    29	    slug: `s${item_order}`,
    30	    item_order,
    31	    carried: 3,
    32	    total: 3,
    33	    percent: 100,
    34	    band: "at-the-standard",
    35	    missed: [],
    36	    decoysCarried: [],
    37	    unjudged: [],
    38	    reciting: false,
    39	    listing: false,
    40	    coloured: true,
    41	    colourSpecific: true,
    42	    dead_air_seconds: 0,
    43	    spoken: "she said something here",
    44	    ...over,
    45	  };
    46	}
    47	
    48	const SLOTS = 25;
    49	function run(seconds: number, createdAt: string, gaps: number[] = [], silent: number[] = []) {
    50	  return {
    51	    assignment_id: `assign-${seconds}`,
    52	    attempt_no: 1,
    53	    source_section: "PT105.S2",
    54	    graded_at: createdAt,
    55	    created_at: createdAt,
    56	    stimuli: Array.from({ length: SLOTS }, (_, i) => {
    57	      const n = i + 1;
    58	      if (silent.includes(n)) return stim(n, { spoken: "", carried: 0, percent: 0 });
    59	      if (gaps.includes(n)) return stim(n, { ungraded: "judge returned nothing" });
    60	      return stim(n);
    61	    }),
    62	    section: {
    63	      accommodation: "standard",
    64	      goal_minutes: 25,
    65	      total_seconds: seconds,
    66	      within_time: seconds <= 1500,
    67	      within_goal: seconds <= 1500,
    68	      mean_coverage: 91,
    69	      stimuli_graded: SLOTS,
    70	      stimuli_in_section: SLOTS,
    71	      reciting_count: 0,
    72	      listing_count: 0,
    73	      uncoloured_count: 0,
    74	      worst_dead_air_seconds: 4,
    75	      dead_air_threshold_seconds: 8,
    76	      decoy_failures: 0,
    77	      clock: {},
    78	    },
    79	  };
    80	}
    81	
    82	const FIXTURE = [
    83	  run(1450, "2026-09-02T09:00:00Z"),
    84	  run(1665, "2026-09-01T09:00:00Z"),
    85	  run(1862, "2026-08-31T09:00:00Z", [9, 17], [4, 21]),
    86	];
    87	
    88	// Stubbed before the Supabase client is imported below, so the real client
    89	// builds the real request and this answers it.
    90	const realFetch = window.fetch.bind(window);
    91	window.fetch = (async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    92	  const url = typeof input === "string" ? input : input.toString();
    93	  if (url.includes("activity_section_grades")) {
    94	    if (state === "failed") {
    95	      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ code: "42501", message: "permission denied" }), {
    96	        status: 401,
    97	        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    98	      });
    99	    }
   100	    const body =
   101	      state === "empty" ? [] : state === "oneRun" ? [FIXTURE[0]] : FIXTURE;
   102	    return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
   103	      status: 200,
   104	      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
   105	    });
   106	  }
   107	  return realFetch(input, init);
   108	}) as typeof window.fetch;
   109	
   110	if (theme === "dark") document.documentElement.classList.add("dark");
   111	
   112	/*
   113	 * The library door renders the pure list, not the router-wired panel: the
   114	 * harness has no router and no session, and the half worth looking at is the
   115	 * page, not the navigation.
   116	 */
   117	async function bootLibrary() {
   118	  const { SectionLibraryLaunch } = await import(
   119	    "../features/activity-centre/components/SectionLibraryLaunch"
   120	  );
   121	  const { SectionLibrary } = await import(
   122	    "../features/activity-centre/components/SectionLibrary"
   123	  );
   124	  await import("../index.css");
   125	  document.body.style.margin = "0";
   126	  const choices = [
   127	    { id: "a1", source_section: "PT105.S2", goal_minutes: { standard: 25 } },
   128	    { id: "a2", source_section: "PT102.S3", goal_minutes: { standard: 25 } },
   129	    { id: "a3", source_section: "PT1.S3", goal_minutes: { standard: 25 } },
   130	  ];
   131	  const attempts = [
   132	    { id: "r1", mode: "section", source_section: "PT105.S2", status: "completed",
   133	      created_at: "2026-09-02T09:00:00Z", completed_at: "2026-09-02T09:25:00Z" },
   134	    { id: "r2", mode: "section", source_section: "PT105.S2", status: "completed",
   135	      created_at: "2026-08-31T09:00:00Z", completed_at: "2026-08-31T09:31:00Z" },
   136	    { id: "r3", mode: "section", source_section: "PT102.S3", status: "in_progress",
   137	      created_at: "2026-09-01T09:00:00Z", completed_at: null },
   138	    /*
   139	     * A section Joshua assigned that she has not opened.
   140	     *
   141	     * Yahunathan's case on 2026-09-08: eight of these, one per section, each
   142	     * with its own message. It must read "Not run yet." and "Start", never
   143	     * "Attempt 1 - not finished" and "Run it again".
   144	     */
   145	    { id: "r4", mode: "section", source_section: "PT1.S3", status: "assigned",
   146	      created_at: "2026-09-08T15:15:00Z", started_at: null, completed_at: null },
   147	  ];
   148	  const grades = [
   149	    { assignment_id: "r1", attempt_no: 1, source_section: "PT105.S2",
   150	      graded_at: "2026-09-02T09:40:00Z", section: { mean_coverage: 91 } },
   151	  ];
   152	  createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(
   153	    <SectionLibraryLaunch
   154	      title="Read, Recall, Colour"
   155	      subtitle={null}
   156	      instructions={sectionInstructions as unknown as ActivityInstructions}
   157	      onExit={() => console.log("[preview] exit")}
   158	    >
   159	      <SectionLibrary
   160	        attempts={attempts as never}
   161	        onStart={(id) => console.log("[preview] start", id)}
   162	        onOpenAttempt={(id) => console.log("[preview] open", id)}
   163	        loadChoices={() => Promise.resolve(choices as never)}
   164	        loadGrades={() => Promise.resolve(grades as never)}
   165	        beginAttempt={(id) => Promise.resolve(`new-${id}`)}
   166	      />
   167	    </SectionLibraryLaunch>,
   168	  );
   169	}
   170	
   171	async function boot() {
   172	  if (state === "library") return bootLibrary();
   173	  const { SectionLaunch } = await import(
   174	    "../features/activity-centre/components/SectionLaunch"
   175	  );
   176	  await import("../index.css");
   177	  document.body.style.margin = "0";
   178	  createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(
   179	    <SectionLaunch
   180	      title="Read, Recall, Colour"
   181	      subtitle="PT105 Section 2"
   182	      instructions={sectionInstructions as unknown as ActivityInstructions}
   183	      itemCount={SLOTS}
   184	      timeGoalMinutes={25}
   185	      sourceSection="PT105.S2"
   186	      studentId="preview-student"
   187	      showHistory={state !== "noHistory"}
   188	      onStart={() => console.log("[preview] start")}
   189	    />,
   190	  );
   191	}
   192	
   193	void boot();

     1	/* The Read, Recall, Colour door.
     2	 *
     3	 * One evening, in two registers. The hero is the runner's own indigo lifted
     4	 * into a dusk sky over a painted meadow, cut into depth planes that move at
     5	 * their own rates as she scrolls; everything under it sits on cream paper in
     6	 * the same light. The method is performed before it is explained, the four
     7	 * steps are the next thing she meets, and the sections she can run sit
     8	 * directly beneath.
     9	 *
    10	 * Every value is scoped to `.sl`. Nothing here touches the runner
    11	 * (`recall/section.css`) or the shell outside this door. Class names beginning
    12	 * `sl-` are the door's contract with its tests and its two wrappers; `rd-`
    13	 * names are the design and may change freely.
    14	 */
    15	
    16	.sl {
    17	  /* The hero panel's ink. Named so a rule that borrows it onto cream is
    18	     visible as a mistake instead of reading as an ordinary colour. */
    19	  --rd-on-dark: #fff;
    20	  --rd-cream: #f6f3ea;
    21	  --rd-paper: #fffdf8;
    22	  --rd-paper-2: #f3efe4;
    23	  --rd-ink: #16132b;
    24	  --rd-ink-2: #46435f;
    25	  --rd-ink-3: #6a6784;
    26	  --rd-line: #e3ded2;
    27	  --rd-line-2: #ede9df;
    28	  --rd-indigo: #1a1635;
    29	  --rd-indigo-2: #2a2450;
    30	  --rd-lavender: #c9c3ec;
    31	  --rd-apricot: #f3c9a0;
    32	  --rd-apricot-light: #ffe8c8;
    33	  --rd-marigold: #f2b544;
    34	  --rd-marigold-ink: #6e4600;
    35	  --rd-marigold-wash: rgba(242, 181, 68, 0.26);
    36	  --rd-emerald: #0e6f4d;
    37	  --rd-emerald-wash: rgba(14, 111, 77, 0.11);
    38	  --rd-display: "Fraunces", "Iowan Old Style", "Times New Roman", serif;
    39	  --rd-sans: "Inter", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
    40	  --rd-mono: "IBM Plex Mono", ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace;
    41	  --rd-ease: cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
    42	  --rd-radius: 22px;
    43	  --rd-radius-panel: 28px;
    44	
    45	  min-height: 100%;
    46	  background: var(--rd-cream);
    47	  color: var(--rd-ink);
    48	  font-family: var(--rd-sans);
    49	  font-size: 16px;
    50	  line-height: 1.5;
    51	  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    52	  accent-color: var(--rd-ink);
    53	  caret-color: var(--rd-marigold-ink);
    54	  scrollbar-color: #c9c3b6 var(--rd-cream);
    55	}
    56	.sl ::selection {
    57	  background: var(--rd-marigold-wash);
    58	  color: var(--rd-ink);
    59	}
    60	.sl :focus-visible {
    61	  outline: 2px solid var(--rd-ink);
    62	  outline-offset: 3px;
    63	  box-shadow: none;
    64	  border-radius: 6px;
    65	}
    66	.sl em {
    67	  font-style: italic;
    68	}
    69

    71	.sl-label {

    79	/* =============================================================== hero */
    80	
    81	.rd-hero {
    82	  padding: 14px 14px 0;
    83	}
    84	.rd-hero-panel {
    85	  position: relative;

    96	/* The scene: five planes and a stage, each its own layer. */
    97	.mw {

   202	.rd-hero-copy {

   204	  z-index: 8;

   211	.sl-chip {
   212	  display: inline-flex;
   213	  align-items: center;
   214	  gap: 8px;
   215	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
   216	  font-size: 11px;
   217	  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
   218	  text-transform: uppercase;
   219	  color: var(--rd-on-dark);
   220	  background: rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.34);

   223	  padding: 7px 14px 7px 12px;

   226	.sl-title {
   227	  margin: 22px 0 0;
   228	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
   229	  font-weight: 400;
   230	  font-variation-settings: "opsz" 144, "SOFT" 30, "WONK" 0;
   231	  font-size: clamp(46px, 6.6vw, 96px);
   232	  line-height: 0.98;
   233	  letter-spacing: -0.03em;

   262	.sl-start {
   263	  appearance: none;
   264	  border: 0;
   265	  cursor: pointer;
   266	  display: inline-flex;
   267	  align-items: center;
   268	  gap: 10px;
   269	  min-height: 52px;
   270	  padding: 0 26px;
   271	  font: inherit;
   272	  font-size: 15px;
   273	  font-weight: 600;
   274	  letter-spacing: -0.005em;
   275	  color: var(--rd-cream);
   276	  background: var(--rd-ink);
   277	  border-radius: 9999px;
   278	  box-shadow:
   279	    0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12),
   280	    0 18px 30px -18px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.7);
   281	  transition:
   282	    transform 0.35s var(--rd-ease),
   283	    background 0.2s ease,
   284	    box-shadow 0.35s var(--rd-ease);

   286	.sl-start:hover {

   290	    0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16),
   291	    0 24px 36px -18px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.75);
   292	}

   294	  transform: translateY(0);
   295	}

   299	.sl-start:hover svg {
   300	  transform: translateY(2px);
   301	}
   302	.sl-brief {
   303	  appearance: none;
   304	  border: 0;

   340	/* =============================================================== demo */
   341	
   342	.rd-demo {
   343	  width: min(800px, 100%);
   344	  background: var(--rd-paper);
   345	  color: var(--rd-ink);
   346	  border-radius: 18px;
   347	  overflow: hidden;
   348	  text-align: left;
   349	  box-shadow:
   350	    0 0 0 1px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.1),
   351	    0 40px 70px -34px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.65),
   352	    0 12px 24px -16px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.4);
   353	}
   354	.rd-demo-bar {
   355	  display: flex;
   356	  align-items: center;
   357	  gap: 12px;
   358	  padding: 10px 14px;
   359	  background: #fbf9f3;
   360	  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
   361	}
   362	.rd-demo-dots {
   363	  display: inline-flex;
   364	  gap: 6px;
   365	}
   366	.rd-demo-dots i {
   367	  width: 9px;
   368	  height: 9px;
   369	  border-radius: 50%;
   370	  background: #e0ddd3;
   371	}
   372	.rd-demo-dots i:first-child {
   373	  background: #f0876f;
   374	}
   375	.rd-demo-dots i:nth-child(2) {
   376	  background: #f2c65a;
   377	}
   378	.rd-demo-dots i:nth-child(3) {
   379	  background: #7fcb8e;
   380	}
   381	.rd-demo-title {
   382	  font-size: 13px;
   383	  font-weight: 600;
   384	  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
   385	}
   386	.rd-demo-step {
   387	  margin-left: auto;
   388	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
   389	  font-size: 10.5px;
   390	  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
   391	  text-transform: uppercase;
   392	  color: var(--rd-marigold-ink);
   393	  background: var(--rd-marigold-wash);
   394	  border-radius: 9999px;
   395	  padding: 4px 10px;
   396	  white-space: nowrap;
   397	}
   398	.rd-demo-rec {
   399	  display: inline-flex;
   400	  align-items: center;
   401	  gap: 7px;
   402	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
   403	  font-size: 11.5px;
   404	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
   405	  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
   406	}
   407	.rd-rec-dot {
   408	  width: 7px;
   409	  height: 7px;
   410	  border-radius: 50%;
   411	  background: #d2402e;
   412	  opacity: 0.45;
   413	}
   414	.rd-rec-dot[data-live] {
   415	  opacity: 1;
   416	  animation: rd-blink 1.3s ease-in-out infinite;
   417	}
   418	@keyframes rd-blink {
   419	  50% {
   420	    opacity: 0.25;
   421	  }
   422	}
   423	.rd-demo-play {
   424	  appearance: none;
   425	  cursor: pointer;
   426	  display: inline-flex;
   427	  align-items: center;
   428	  justify-content: center;
   429	  width: 28px;
   430	  height: 28px;
   431	  border-radius: 50%;
   432	  border: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
   433	  background: var(--rd-paper);
   434	  color: var(--rd-ink);
   435	  padding: 0;
   436	  transition: background 0.2s ease;
   437	}
   438	.rd-demo-play:hover:not(:disabled) {
   439	  background: var(--rd-paper-2);
   440	}
   441	.rd-demo-play:disabled {
   442	  opacity: 0.4;
   443	  cursor: default;
   444	}
   445	.rd-demo-body {
   446	  display: grid;
   447	  grid-template-columns: 1.12fr 1fr;
   448	}
   449	.rd-paper {
   450	  position: relative;
   451	  display: flex;
   452	  flex-direction: column;
   453	  min-height: 262px;
   454	  padding: 18px 20px 16px;
   455	  border-right: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
   456	}
   457	.rd-demo-label {
   458	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
   459	  font-size: 10px;
   460	  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
   461	  text-transform: uppercase;
   462	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
   463	}
   464	.rd-stim {
   465	  margin: 12px 0 0;
   466	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
   467	  font-variation-settings: "opsz" 18;
   468	  font-size: 16.5px;
   469	  line-height: 1.55;
   470	  color: var(--rd-ink);
   471	  transition:
   472	    opacity 0.9s var(--rd-ease),
   473	    filter 0.9s var(--rd-ease),
   474	    transform 0.9s var(--rd-ease);
   475	}
   476	.rd-paper[data-gone] .rd-stim {
   477	  opacity: 0;
   478	  filter: blur(10px);
   479	  transform: translateY(-6px);
   480	}
   481	.rd-stim-line {
   482	  border-radius: 4px;
   483	  padding: 1px 2px;
   484	  box-decoration-break: clone;
   485	  -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;
   486	  transition:
   487	    background 0.45s ease,
   488	    color 0.45s ease;
   489	}
   490	.rd-stim-line[data-active] {
   491	  background: var(--rd-marigold-wash);
   492	}
   493	.rd-stim-line[data-done] {
   494	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
   495	}
   496	.rd-gone {
   497	  position: absolute;
   498	  left: 20px;
   499	  right: 20px;
   500	  top: 46%;
   501	  transform: translateY(-50%);
   502	  margin: 0;
   503	  padding: 0 12px;
   504	  text-align: center;
   505	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
   506	  font-style: italic;
   507	  font-size: 17px;
   508	  line-height: 1.4;
   509	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
   510	}
   511	.rd-keyrow {
   512	  display: flex;
   513	  align-items: center;
   514	  gap: 10px;
   515	  margin-top: auto;
   516	  padding-top: 16px;

   518	.rd-key {
   519	  display: inline-block;
   520	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
   521	  font-size: 11px;

   541	}

   544	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
   545	}

   585	  gap: 2px;

   601	.rd-wave[data-live] i {
   602	  animation: rd-wave 0.85s ease-in-out infinite alternate;
   603	}
   604	@keyframes rd-wave {
   605	  from {
   606	    height: 3px;
   607	  }
   608	  to {
   609	    height: 12px;
   610	  }

   612	.rd-caret {

95+700	.sl-beat:hover {
96+700	  transform: translateY(-4px);
97+700	  box-shadow: 0 30px 44px -30px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.4);
98+700	}
99+700	.sl-beat[aria-selected="true"] {
100+700	  border-color: var(--rd-ink);
101+700	  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--rd-ink);
102+700	}
103+700	.rd-step-n {
104+700	  margin: 22px 24px 0;
105+700	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
106+700	  font-size: 11px;
107+700	  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
108+700	  text-transform: uppercase;
109+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
110+700	}
111+700	.sl-beat .sl-l {
112+700	  display: block;
113+700	  margin: 8px 24px 0;
114+700	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
115+700	  font-weight: 400;
116+700	  font-size: 28px;
117+700	  line-height: 1.1;
118+700	  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
119+700	  color: var(--rd-ink);
120+700	}
121+700	.sl-beat .sl-d {
122+700	  display: -webkit-box;
123+700	  -webkit-box-orient: vertical;
124+700	  -webkit-line-clamp: 4;
125+700	  overflow: hidden;
126+700	  margin: 10px 24px 0;
127+700	  font-size: 14.5px;
128+700	  line-height: 1.55;
129+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
130+700	}
131+700	
132+700	/* The four small scenes. */
133+700	.rd-scene {
134+700	  position: relative;
135+700	  height: 172px;
136+700	  overflow: hidden;
137+700	  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
138+700	}
139+700	.rd-sc-read {
140+700	  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #d6cff0 0%, #ecd8d8 60%, #f5d9be 100%);
141+700	}
142+700	.rd-sc-tell {
143+700	  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #2a2450 0%, #5b4f8f 70%, #a89fe0 100%);
144+700	}
145+700	.rd-sc-colour {
146+700	  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f5d9be 0%, #f3c9a0 55%, #e8c9d3 100%);
147+700	}
148+700	.rd-sc-recall {
149+700	  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #b9b0dd 0%, #d8cfe6 60%, #eadfd8 100%);
150+700	}
151+700	.rd-sc-page {
152+700	  position: absolute;
153+700	  left: 26px;
154+700	  right: 26px;
155+700	  bottom: -14px;
156+700	  height: 128px;
157+700	  display: flex;
158+700	  flex-direction: column;
159+700	  gap: 13px;
160+700	  padding: 22px 20px;
161+700	  background: var(--rd-paper);
162+700	  border-radius: 12px 12px 0 0;
163+700	  box-shadow: 0 -10px 32px -16px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.45);
164+700	}
165+700	.rd-sc-line {
166+700	  display: block;
167+700	  height: 9px;
168+700	  border-radius: 5px;
169+700	  background: #e4dfd3;
170+700	}
171+700	.rd-sc-sweep {
172+700	  position: absolute;
173+700	  left: 18px;
174+700	  top: 20px;
175+700	  width: 88%;
176+700	  height: 13px;
177+700	  border-radius: 6px;
178+700	  background: var(--rd-marigold-wash);
179+700	  transform-origin: left center;
180+700	  animation: rd-sweep 3s var(--rd-ease) infinite;
181+700	}
182+700	@keyframes rd-sweep {
183+700	  0% {
184+700	    transform: scaleX(0);
185+700	    opacity: 1;
186+700	  }
187+700	  50% {
188+700	    transform: scaleX(1);
189+700	    opacity: 1;
190+700	  }
191+700	  80% {
192+700	    transform: scaleX(1);
193+700	    opacity: 0;
194+700	  }
195+700	  100% {
196+700	    transform: scaleX(0);
197+700	    opacity: 0;
198+700	  }
199+700	}
200+700	.rd-sc-bubble {
201+700	  position: absolute;
202+700	  left: 24px;
203+700	  top: 28px;
204+700	  display: flex;
205+700	  align-items: center;
206+700	  gap: 10px;
207+700	  padding: 11px 15px;
208+700	  background: var(--rd-indigo);
209+700	  color: var(--rd-apricot-light);
210+700	  border-radius: 14px 14px 14px 4px;
211+700	  box-shadow: 0 16px 28px -16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
212+700	}
213+700	.rd-sc-bubble-text {
214+700	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
215+700	  font-style: italic;
216+700	  font-size: 14px;
217+700	  color: var(--rd-on-dark);
218+700	}
219+700	.rd-sc-page-turned {
220+700	  left: 46%;
221+700	  right: -30px;
222+700	  bottom: -46px;
223+700	  transform: rotate(-7deg);
224+700	  opacity: 0.92;
225+700	}
226+700	.rd-sc-stroke {
227+700	  position: absolute;
228+700	  left: 8%;
229+700	  top: 40%;
230+700	  width: 84%;
231+700	  height: 34px;
232+700	  background: var(--rd-marigold);
233+700	  border-radius: 18px 22px 20px 16px / 30px 26px 32px 28px;
234+700	  transform: rotate(-2.5deg);
235+700	  transform-origin: left center;
236+700	  animation: rd-stroke 3.4s var(--rd-ease) infinite;
237+700	}
238+700	@keyframes rd-stroke {
239+700	  0% {
240+700	    transform: rotate(-2.5deg) scaleX(0);
241+700	  }
242+700	  45%,
243+700	  85% {
244+700	    transform: rotate(-2.5deg) scaleX(1);
245+700	    opacity: 1;
246+700	  }
247+700	  100% {
248+700	    transform: rotate(-2.5deg) scaleX(1);
249+700	    opacity: 0;
250+700	  }
251+700	}
252+700	.rd-sc-chip {
253+700	  position: absolute;
254+700	  left: 26px;
255+700	  bottom: 24px;
256+700	  padding: 10px 14px 11px;
257+700	  background: var(--rd-paper);
258+700	  border-radius: 12px 12px 4px 12px;
259+700	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
260+700	  font-style: italic;
261+700	  font-size: 15px;
262+700	  color: var(--rd-ink);
263+700	  box-shadow: 0 16px 28px -18px rgba(110, 70, 0, 0.55);
264+700	}
265+700	.rd-sc-chip-head {
266+700	  display: block;
267+700	  margin-bottom: 2px;
268+700	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
269+700	  font-style: normal;
270+700	  font-size: 10px;
271+700	  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
272+700	  text-transform: uppercase;
273+700	  color: var(--rd-marigold-ink);
274+700	}
275+700	.rd-sc-page-fading .rd-sc-line {
276+700	  animation: rd-fade 3.6s ease-in-out infinite;
277+700	}
278+700	.rd-sc-page-fading .rd-sc-line:nth-child(2) {
279+700	  animation-delay: 0.15s;
280+700	}
281+700	.rd-sc-page-fading .rd-sc-line:nth-child(3) {
282+700	  animation-delay: 0.3s;
283+700	}
284+700	.rd-sc-page-fading .rd-sc-line:nth-child(4) {
285+700	  animation-delay: 0.45s;
286+700	}
287+700	@keyframes rd-fade {
288+700	  0%,
289+700	  30% {
290+700	    opacity: 1;
291+700	    filter: blur(0);
292+700	  }
293+700	  55%,
294+700	  85% {
295+700	    opacity: 0;
296+700	    filter: blur(4px);
297+700	  }
298+700	  100% {
299+700	    opacity: 1;
300+700	    filter: blur(0);
301+700	  }
302+700	}
303+700	.rd-sc-key {
304+700	  position: absolute;
305+700	  right: 24px;
306+700	  bottom: 24px;
307+700	  padding: 6px 15px;
308+700	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
309+700	  font-size: 11px;
310+700	  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
311+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
312+700	  background: var(--rd-on-dark);
313+700	  border-radius: 8px;
314+700	  box-shadow:
315+700	    0 3px 0 #cfc9bd,
316+700	    0 0 0 1px #e3ded2,
317+700	    0 12px 20px -12px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.5);
318+700	  animation: rd-press 3.6s ease-in-out infinite;
319+700	}
320+700	@keyframes rd-press {
321+700	  0%,
322+700	  22% {
323+700	    transform: translateY(0);
324+700	    box-shadow:
325+700	      0 3px 0 #cfc9bd,
326+700	      0 0 0 1px #e3ded2,
327+700	      0 12px 20px -12px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.5);
328+700	  }
329+700	  28%,
330+700	  40% {
331+700	    transform: translateY(3px);
332+700	    box-shadow:
333+700	      0 0 0 #cfc9bd,
334+700	      0 0 0 1px #cfc9bd,
335+700	      0 4px 10px -8px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.5);
336+700	  }
337+700	  46%,
338+700	  100% {
339+700	    transform: translateY(0);
340+700	    box-shadow:
341+700	      0 3px 0 #cfc9bd,
342+700	      0 0 0 1px #e3ded2,
343+700	      0 12px 20px -12px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.5);
344+700	  }
345+700	}
346+700	
347+700	/* ---- the drawer under the steps ---- */
348+700	.sl-drawer {
349+700	  margin-top: 18px;
350+700	  background: var(--rd-paper);
351+700	  border: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
352+700	  border-radius: var(--rd-radius);
353+700	  overflow: hidden;
354+700	  scroll-margin-top: 20px;
355+700	}
356+700	.sl-dr-tabs {
357+700	  display: flex;
358+700	  align-items: center;
359+700	  gap: 24px;
360+700	  padding: 14px 28px 0;
361+700	  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
362+700	}
363+700	.sl-dr-tab {
364+700	  appearance: none;
365+700	  border: 0;
366+700	  background: none;
367+700	  cursor: pointer;
368+700	  font: inherit;
369+700	  font-size: 14px;
370+700	  font-weight: 500;
371+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
372+700	  padding: 8px 0 14px;
373+700	  box-shadow: inset 0 -2px 0 transparent;
374+700	  transition:
375+700	    color 0.2s ease,
376+700	    box-shadow 0.2s ease;
377+700	}
378+700	.sl-dr-tab[aria-selected="true"] {
379+700	  color: var(--rd-ink);
380+700	  box-shadow: inset 0 -2px 0 var(--rd-ink);
381+700	}
382+700	.sl-dr-note {
383+700	  margin-left: auto;
384+700	  font-size: 13px;
385+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
386+700	}
387+700	.sl-dr-view {
388+700	  display: grid;
389+700	  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 240px;
390+700	  gap: 32px;
391+700	  padding: 30px 28px 32px;
392+700	}
393+700	.sl-dr-view[data-pane="beat"] {
394+700	  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
395+700	}
396+700	.sl-dr-title {
397+700	  margin: 10px 0 0;
398+700	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
399+700	  font-weight: 400;
400+700	  font-size: clamp(28px, 3vw, 38px);
401+700	  line-height: 1.05;
402+700	  letter-spacing: -0.024em;
403+700	  color: var(--rd-ink);
404+700	}
405+700	.sl-dr-body {
406+700	  margin: 14px 0 0;
407+700	  max-width: 62ch;
408+700	  font-size: 17px;
409+700	  line-height: 1.6;
410+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
411+700	  text-wrap: pretty;
412+700	}
413+700	.rd-dr-nav {
414+700	  display: flex;
415+700	  flex-wrap: wrap;
416+700	  gap: 10px;
417+700	  margin-top: 24px;
418+700	}
419+700	.sl-close {
420+700	  appearance: none;
421+700	  cursor: pointer;
422+700	  display: inline-flex;
423+700	  align-items: center;
424+700	  gap: 8px;
425+700	  min-height: 44px;
426+700	  padding: 0 18px;
427+700	  font: inherit;
428+700	  font-size: 14px;
429+700	  font-weight: 500;
430+700	  color: var(--rd-ink);
431+700	  background: var(--rd-paper);
432+700	  border: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
433+700	  border-radius: 9999px;
434+700	  transition:
435+700	    background 0.2s ease,
436+700	    border-color 0.2s ease;
437+700	}
438+700	.sl-close:hover {
439+700	  background: var(--rd-paper-2);
440+700	  border-color: #cfc9bd;
441+700	}
442+700	.sl-dr-side {
443+700	  display: flex;
444+700	  flex-direction: column;
445+700	  align-items: flex-start;
446+700	  gap: 10px;
447+700	  align-self: start;
448+700	  position: sticky;
449+700	  top: 24px;
450+700	}
451+700	.sl-essay {
452+700	  max-width: 68ch;
453+700	}
454+700	/* `.sl-essay p` outranks a bare class, so the lede was rendering at body
455+700	   size. Written at the same specificity so it leads. */
456+700	.rd-lede,
457+700	.sl-essay p.rd-lede {
458+700	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
459+700	  font-style: italic;
460+700	  font-size: 20px;
461+700	  line-height: 1.45;
462+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
463+700	}
464+700	.sl-essay p {
465+700	  margin: 0 0 14px;
466+700	  font-size: 16.5px;
467+700	  line-height: 1.65;
468+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
469+700	  text-wrap: pretty;
470+700	}
471+700	.sl-essay p strong {
472+700	  color: var(--rd-ink);
473+700	  font-weight: 600;
474+700	}
475+700	.sl-essay h3,
476+700	.sl-essay h4 {
477+700	  margin: 36px 0 10px;
478+700	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
479+700	  font-weight: 400;
480+700	  font-size: 26px;
481+700	  line-height: 1.15;
482+700	  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
483+700	  color: var(--rd-ink);
484+700	  text-wrap: balance;
485+700	}
486+700	.sl-essay section:first-of-type h4 {
487+700	  margin-top: 8px;
488+700	}
489+700	.sl-essay ol {
490+700	  margin: 10px 0 16px;
491+700	  padding-left: 22px;
492+700	}
493+700	.sl-essay li {
494+700	  margin-bottom: 8px;
495+700	  font-size: 16.5px;
496+700	  line-height: 1.6;
497+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
498+700	}
499+700	.sl-essay li strong {
500+700	  color: var(--rd-ink);
501+700	  font-weight: 600;
502+700	}
503+700	
504+700	/* ============================================================= counts */
505+700	
506+700	.rd-counts-grid {
507+700	  display: grid;
508+700	  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
509+700	  gap: 16px;
510+700	}
511+700	.rd-count {
512+700	  position: relative;
513+700	  display: flex;
514+700	  flex-direction: column;
515+700	  padding: 26px 26px 28px;
516+700	  background: var(--rd-paper);
517+700	  border: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
518+700	  border-radius: var(--rd-radius);
519+700	}
520+700	.rd-count[data-tone="good"] {
521+700	  background: var(--rd-indigo);
522+700	  border-color: var(--rd-indigo);
523+700	  color: var(--rd-on-dark);
524+700	  box-shadow: 0 34px 60px -34px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.7);
525+700	}
526+700	.rd-count-tag {
527+700	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
528+700	  font-size: 11px;
529+700	  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
530+700	  text-transform: uppercase;
531+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
532+700	}
533+700	.rd-count[data-tone="good"] .rd-count-tag {
534+700	  color: var(--rd-apricot-light);
535+700	}
536+700	.rd-count-quote {
537+700	  margin: 18px 0 0;
538+700	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
539+700	  font-size: 22px;
540+700	  line-height: 1.32;
541+700	  letter-spacing: -0.012em;
542+700	  text-wrap: pretty;
543+700	}
544+700	.rd-count-note {
545+700	  margin: auto 0 0;
546+700	  padding-top: 20px;
547+700	  font-size: 14.5px;
548+700	  line-height: 1.5;
549+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
550+700	}
551+700	.rd-count[data-tone="good"] .rd-count-note {
552+700	  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.82);
553+700	}
554+700	
555+700	/* =============================================================== slab */
556+700	
557+700	.rd-slab {
558+700	  scroll-margin-top: 20px;
559+700	}
560+700	.sl-runs {
561+700	  display: grid;
562+700	  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 340px;
563+700	  gap: 16px;
564+700	  align-items: start;
565+700	}
566+700	.sl-runs > div:first-child {
567+700	  background: var(--rd-paper);
568+700	  border: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
569+700	  border-radius: var(--rd-radius);
570+700	  padding: 26px 28px 14px;
571+700	}
572+700	.sl-aside {
573+700	  padding: 24px 26px 26px;
574+700	  background: var(--rd-paper);
575+700	  border: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
576+700	  border-radius: var(--rd-radius);
577+700	}
578+700	.sl-aside p {
579+700	  margin: 12px 0 0;
580+700	  font-size: 15px;
581+700	  line-height: 1.55;
582+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
583+700	}
584+700	.sl-aside strong {
585+700	  color: var(--rd-ink);
586+700	  font-weight: 600;
587+700	}
588+700	.sl-key {
589+700	  display: inline-block;
590+700	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
591+700	  font-size: 11px;
592+700	  padding: 1px 7px;
593+700	  border-radius: 5px;
594+700	  background: var(--rd-on-dark);
595+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
596+700	  box-shadow:
597+700	    0 2px 0 #d9d3c6,
598+700	    0 0 0 1px var(--rd-line);
599+700	}
600+700	.sl-note {
601+700	  margin: 0;
602+700	  font-size: 14px;
603+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
604+700	}
605+700	.sl-note[data-tone="bad"] {
606+700	  color: var(--rd-ink);
607+700	  font-weight: 500;
608+700	}
609+700	
610+700	/* ---- the section rows ---- */
611+700	.sl-sections {
612+700	  display: flex;
613+700	  flex-direction: column;
614+700	}
615+700	.sl-section {
616+700	  display: grid;
617+700	  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
618+700	  gap: 16px;
619+700	  align-items: center;
620+700	  padding: 22px 0;
621+700	  border-top: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
622+700	}
623+700	.sl-section:first-child {
624+700	  border-top: 0;
625+700	  padding-top: 8px;
626+700	}
627+700	.sl-sec-body {
628+700	  min-width: 0;
629+700	}
630+700	.sl-sec-line {
631+700	  display: flex;
632+700	  align-items: baseline;
633+700	  gap: 14px;
634+700	}
635+700	.sl-sec-name {
636+700	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
637+700	  font-weight: 400;
638+700	  font-size: 30px;
639+700	  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
640+700	  line-height: 1;
641+700	  color: var(--rd-ink);
642+700	  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
643+700	}
644+700	.sl-sec-clock {
645+700	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
646+700	  font-size: 12px;
647+700	  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
648+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
649+700	}
650+700	.sl-sec-attempts {
651+700	  list-style: none;
652+700	  margin: 10px 0 0;
653+700	  padding: 0;
654+700	  display: flex;
655+700	  flex-direction: column;
656+700	  gap: 4px;
657+700	}
658+700	.sl-sec-body > .sl-note {
659+700	  margin-top: 10px;
660+700	}
661+700	/* A past Attempt is the way back to its Report Card, so it is a control and
662+700	   not a footnote. It was 13.5px muted text with a dotted underline and no
663+700	   padding, which is what a caption looks like, and the student it was built
664+700	   for read the score off it and asked where the graded responses were
665+700	   (Celia, 2026-09-08). Bordered, full width, and 44px of target. */
666+700	.sl-sec-attempt {
667+700	  align-items: center;
668+700	  background: var(--rd-paper, #fff);
669+700	  border: 1px solid var(--rd-line, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12));
670+700	  border-radius: 10px;
671+700	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
672+700	  cursor: pointer;
673+700	  display: flex;
674+700	  flex-wrap: wrap;
675+700	  font: inherit;
676+700	  font-size: 13.5px;
677+700	  gap: 4px 12px;

358+1400	/* Motion is a courtesy, never the mechanism. Every state above is reachable
359+1400	   with this on; continuous loops stop rather than speed up. */
360+1400	@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
361+1400	  .sl *,
362+1400	  .sl *::before,
363+1400	  .sl *::after {
364+1400	    animation: none !important;
365+1400	    transition: none !important;
366+1400	  }
367+1400	  .mw-layer,
368+1400	  .mw-stage,
369+1400	  .rd-hero-copy {
370+1400	    will-change: auto;
371+1400	  }
372+1400	}
373+1400	
374+1400	/* ------------------------------------------------------- the briefing page
375+1400	   The instructions on their own page, before anything records. One column,
376+1400	   everything open, Start at the top and again at the foot. */
377+1400	.sl-briefing-page {
378+1400	  max-width: 880px;
379+1400	  margin: 0 auto;
380+1400	  padding: 40px 20px 96px;
381+1400	  display: grid;
382+1400	  gap: 32px;
383+1400	}
384+1400	/* Stacked on purpose. It was space-between, and at this page's width the
385+1400	   title block and the CTA never fit on one line, so the CTA always wrapped
386+1400	   under the tagline: the layout that shipped was accidental. */
387+1400	.sl-bp-head {
388+1400	  display: flex;
389+1400	  flex-direction: column;
390+1400	  gap: 22px;
391+1400	  align-items: flex-start;
392+1400	}
393+1400	/* The notice, un-boxed. It shared fill, hairline and radius with the four step
394+1400	   cards below it, so the one sentence written to lower her shoulders read as a
395+1400	   fifth thing to do. A plain line in ink, the page speaking. */
396+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-notice {
397+1400	  padding: 0;
398+1400	  background: none;
399+1400	  border: 0;
400+1400	  border-radius: 0;
401+1400	  font-size: 17px;
402+1400	  line-height: 1.5;
403+1400	  color: var(--rd-ink);
404+1400	  max-width: 62ch;
405+1400	}
406+1400	/* A landing-page pill on a task page. The lift and outer shadow are the
407+1400	   door's persuade register; on paper the fill darkening is enough. */
408+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-start,
409+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-start:hover,
410+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-start:active {
411+1400	  transform: none;
412+1400	  box-shadow: none;
413+1400	}
414+1400	/* The hero classes are written for the door's dark purple ground: .sl-title is
415+1400	   white at up to 96px, .sl-tagline is white with a drop shadow. This page sits
416+1400	   on paper, so both were being drawn in white on cream. The briefing page uses
417+1400	   the same classes for the same copy, so it restates the colour and the size
418+1400	   here rather than forking them. */
419+1400	/* The focus ring, per control rather than once at the top.
420+1400	   `.sl :focus-visible` sets a ring for the whole world, and loses the cascade on
421+1400	   the two button classes, so Start and Back matched :focus-visible and computed
422+1400	   outline-style: none. A keyboard student could not see where she was on the one
423+1400	   control that begins the run, and Back had no indicator at all.
424+1400	
425+1400	   The colour depends on the ground, and these buttons appear on both: the door
426+1400	   draws them on the dark hero panel, every other surface on cream. */
427+1400	.sl .sl-start:focus-visible,
428+1400	.sl .sl-brief:focus-visible {
429+1400	  outline: 2px solid var(--rd-ink);
430+1400	  outline-offset: 3px;
431+1400	}
432+1400	.rd-hero .sl-start:focus-visible,
433+1400	.rd-hero .sl-brief:focus-visible {
434+1400	  outline-color: var(--rd-cream);
435+1400	}
436+1400	/* 43x44 was under the 44x44 floor, and this is the exit control. */
437+1400	.sl-brief {
438+1400	  min-width: 44px;
439+1400	}
440+1400	
441+1400	/* Two more hero classes drawn in white for the door's dark panel. On paper they
442+1400	   were invisible: the drill's own name, and a Back button nobody could see. */
443+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-chip {
444+1400	  color: var(--rd-ink);
445+1400	  background: var(--rd-paper);
446+1400	  border-color: var(--rd-line);
447+1400	  backdrop-filter: none;
448+1400	}
449+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-brief {
450+1400	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
451+1400	  text-decoration-color: var(--rd-line);
452+1400	}
453+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-brief:hover {
454+1400	  color: var(--rd-ink);
455+1400	  text-decoration-color: var(--rd-ink-2);
456+1400	}
457+1400	/* A heading inside the long version was set larger than the section heading
458+1400	   above it, so the page read as if the essay outranked its own label. */
459+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-bp-essay h3 {
460+1400	  font-size: 20px;
461+1400	  margin-top: 28px;
462+1400	}
463+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-title {
464+1400	  color: var(--rd-ink);
465+1400	  font-size: clamp(30px, 3.4vw, 46px);
466+1400	  line-height: 1.06;
467+1400	  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
468+1400	  margin: 14px 0 0;
469+1400	}
470+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-title em {
471+1400	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
472+1400	}
473+1400	/* .sl-tagline is white with a drop shadow, which is right on the door's dark
474+1400	   hero and unreadable here: this page sits on paper. The briefing page reuses
475+1400	   the class for the same line of copy, so it restates the colour rather than
476+1400	   forking the class. */
477+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-tagline {
478+1400	  margin: 12px 0 0;
479+1400	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
480+1400	  text-shadow: none;
481+1400	}
482+1400	.sl-briefing-page .sl-tagline strong {
483+1400	  color: var(--rd-ink);
484+1400	}
485+1400	/* The long version, closed by default. */
486+1400	.sl-bp-more > summary {
487+1400	  cursor: pointer;
488+1400	  list-style: none;
489+1400	  padding: 14px 0;
490+1400	  border-top: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
491+1400	}
492+1400	/* The summary's heading used to inherit body weight, so the only help on the
493+1400	   page looked like a stray sentence beside an uppercase h2. Same treatment as
494+1400	   its sibling, and a drawn chevron instead of a " +" that some readers spoke. */
495+1400	.sl-bp-more > summary {
496+1400	  display: flex;
497+1400	  align-items: center;
498+1400	  gap: 10px;
499+1400	}
500+1400	.sl-bp-more > summary > h2 {
501+1400	  display: inline;
502+1400	  margin: 0;
503+1400	}
504+1400	.sl-bp-more > summary::-webkit-details-marker {
505+1400	  display: none;
506+1400	}
507+1400	.sl-bp-more > summary::after {
508+1400	  content: "";
509+1400	  width: 7px;
510+1400	  height: 7px;
511+1400	  border-right: 1.5px solid var(--rd-ink-2);
512+1400	  border-bottom: 1.5px solid var(--rd-ink-2);
513+1400	  transform: translateY(-2px) rotate(45deg);

     1	import { useEffect } from "react";
     2	import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
     3	import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
     4	import { recoverPendingRecordings, recoverPendingRows } from "../recall/recoverRecordings";
     5	import { useStudentActivities } from "../hooks/useStudentActivities";
     6	import { SectionLibrary } from "./SectionLibrary";
     7	import { SectionLibraryLaunch } from "./SectionLibraryLaunch";
     8	import { SectionProgress } from "./SectionProgress";
     9	import sectionInstructions from "../content/read-recall-colour-section.instructions.json";
    10	import type { ActivityInstructions } from "../api/types";
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * The section list, wired to her own record and to the router.
    14	 *
    15	 * `SectionLibrary` stays pure so it can be rendered in a test without a session
    16	 * or a router. This is the half that knows about both.
    17	 *
    18	 * Starting an Attempt navigates to that Attempt's own assignment and opens the
    19	 * recorder immediately. The library already shows the complete instructions;
    20	 * repeating them on a second page made Start look as though it had not opened
    21	 * the drill at all.
    22	 */
    23	export function SectionLibraryPanel({
    24	  onExit,
    25	  title = "Read, Recall, Colour",
    26	  subtitle = null,
    27	  instructions,
    28	}: {
    29	  onExit?: () => void;
    30	  title?: string;
    31	  subtitle?: string | null;
    32	  /**
    33	   * The drill's own briefing. Defaults to the authored section instructions
    34	   * because the door is not a section and carries no snapshot of its own, and
    35	   * the method it describes is the same one every section is run by.
    36	   */
    37	  instructions?: ActivityInstructions;
    38	}) {
    39	  const navigate = useNavigate();
    40	  const { user } = useAuth();
    41	  const { assignments } = useStudentActivities();
    42	
    43	  /*
    44	   * Send up anything a previous run left on this machine.
    45	   *
    46	   * The runner already does this on the way into an Attempt, which only helps
    47	   * a student who starts another one. This is the door she actually comes back
    48	   * through, so a recording or a set of answers stranded by a crash goes up as
    49	   * soon as she opens the drill -- and is markable from that moment. Silent and
    50	   * best-effort: a failed pass must never keep this page from opening.
    51	   */
    52	  useEffect(() => {
    53	    if (!user?.id) return;
    54	    let cancelled = false;
    55	    void (async () => {
    56	      try {
    57	        await recoverPendingRecordings(user.id);
    58	        if (cancelled) return;
    59	        await recoverPendingRows(user.id);
    60	      } catch {
    61	        // Nothing to say here. The next open tries again.
    62	      }
    63	    })();
    64	    return () => {
    65	      cancelled = true;
    66	    };
    67	  }, [user?.id]);
    68	
    69	  return (
    70	    <SectionLibraryLaunch
    71	      title={title}
    72	      subtitle={subtitle}
    73	      instructions={instructions ?? (sectionInstructions as unknown as ActivityInstructions)}
    74	      onExit={onExit}
    75	    >
    76	      {/*
    77	        * Starting and reopening are the same navigation, on purpose.
    78	        *
    79	        * An Attempt IS its assignment, so its Report Card is at the
    80	        * assignment's own address -- which is what makes a past Attempt
    81	        * reachable from any device at any later date rather than only from
    82	        * the tab that produced it. A completed section routes itself to the
    83	        * card rather than to the runner.
    84	        */}
    85	      <SectionProgress onOpen={(id) => navigate(`/activities/${id}`)} />
    86	
    87	      <SectionLibrary
    88	        attempts={assignments}
    89	        onStart={(id) => navigate(`/activities/${id}`, { state: { startSection: true } })}
    90	        onOpenAttempt={(id) => navigate(`/activities/${id}`)}
    91	      />
    92	    </SectionLibraryLaunch>
    93	  );
    94	}
